📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Communicable diseases intelligence (2018)  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-16  |  DOI: 10.33321/cdi.2026.50.068  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 50

2025年澳大利亚麻疹和风疹基因分型监测计划报告。

2025 Annual Australian Measles and Rubella Genotyping Surveillance Program Report.
作者： Alicia Arnott, Thomas Tran, Marc Ramsperger, Madiyar Zhanduisenov, Chang-Xu Zhang, Leon Caly, Ammar Aziz, Jian-Shu Zhang, Eloise Williams, Katherine A. Bond
摘要中文翻译分子特征分析是澳大利亚麻疹和风疹监测系统的关键组成部分,支持麻疹病例调查、确定感染源,并为维持澳大利亚麻疹和风疹消除状态提供证据。本年度报告总结了2025年澳大利亚麻疹和风疹的分子监测情况,并评估了全基因组测序(WGS)在麻疹基因分型中的应用价值。2025年,澳大利亚报告了181例确认的麻疹病例,较2024年通知的病例数增加了217.5%。检测到麻疹病毒基因型B3和D8,以及21例与风疹相关基因型A病例。基因型B3在上半年占主导地位,主要与从越南输入的病例相关,而基因型D8在下半年占主导地位,主要与从印度尼西亚输入的病例相关。共确认了31例流行病学关联的麻疹病例,其中13例为基因型B3,17例为基因型D8。在麻疹病例调查的背景下,WGS数据在区分能力及与流行病学数据的一致性方面均显著优于传统的基于N450的基因分型方法。在基因型B3中,共享相同N450基序的病毒株之间存在多达33个单核苷酸多态性(SNPs),而在基因型D8中则存在多达36个SNPs;然而,从流行病学关联病例中获得的病毒株之间最多仅存在4个SNPs。尽管2025年全国麻疹病例数量有所增加,但现有的分子和流行病学证据表明,病例主要与输入或输入相关事件有关,没有证据表明存在持续的地方性麻疹传播。风疹活动仍然非常低,通过聚合酶链反应(PCR)检测的157份样本中未发现风疹病毒,全国仅报告了2例病例,且这两例均未提供可用于基因分型的样本。这些发现支持了澳大利亚持续不存在地方性麻疹和风疹传播的结论,并突显了WGS在提升麻疹基因分型和麻疹病例调查中的重要价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
分子特征分析是澳大利亚麻疹和风疹监测系统的关键组成部分,支持麻疹病例调查、确定感染源,并为维持澳大利亚麻疹和风疹消除状态提供证据。
设计与对象
本年度报告总结了2025年澳大利亚麻疹和风疹的分子监测情况,并评估了全基因组测序(WGS)在麻疹基因分型中的应用价值。
核心方法
2025年,澳大利亚报告了181例确认的麻疹病例,较2024年通知的病例数增加了217.5%。风疹活动仍然非常低,通过聚合酶链反应(PCR)检测的157份样本中未发现风疹病毒,全国仅报告了2例病例,且这两例均未提供可用于基因分型的样本。
主要结果
检测到麻疹病毒基因型B3和D8,以及21例与风疹相关基因型A病例。共确认了31例流行病学关联的麻疹病例,其中13例为基因型B3,17例为基因型D8。
解释与创新
基因型B3在上半年占主导地位,主要与从越南输入的病例相关,而基因型D8在下半年占主导地位,主要与从印度尼西亚输入的病例相关。
科研与公卫意义
在麻疹病例调查的背景下,WGS数据在区分能力及与流行病学数据的一致性方面均显著优于传统的基于N450的基因分型方法。这些发现支持了澳大利亚持续不存在地方性麻疹和风疹传播的结论,并突显了WGS在提升麻疹基因分型和麻疹病例调查中的重要价值。
局限与证据强度
在基因型B3中,共享相同N450基序的病毒株之间存在多达33个单核苷酸多态性(SNPs),而在基因型D8中则存在多达36个SNPs;然而,从流行病学关联病例中获得的病毒株之间最多仅存在4个SNPs。

2025 Annual Australian Measles and Rubella Genotyping Surveillance Program Report.

Authors: Alicia Arnott, Thomas Tran, Marc Ramsperger, Madiyar Zhanduisenov, Chang-Xu Zhang, Leon Caly, Ammar Aziz, Jian-Shu Zhang, Eloise Williams, Katherine A. Bond
Original abstract (English)
Molecular characterisation is a critical component of Australia's measles and rubella surveillance systems, supporting outbreak investigations, identifying sources of infection, and providing evidence for the continued maintenance of Australia's measles and rubella elimination status. This annual report summarises measles and rubella molecular epidemiology in Australia during 2025 and evaluates the utility of whole genome sequencing (WGS) for measles surveillance. In 2025, there were 181 confirmed measles cases notified in Australia, representing a 217.5% increase on the number notified in 2024. Measles virus genotypes B3 and D8 were detected, together with 21 vaccine-associated genotype A cases. Genotype B3 predominated during the first half of the year and was largely associated with importations from Vietnam, whereas genotype D8 predominated during the second half of the year and was primarily associated with importations from Indonesia. Thirty-one epidemiologically confirmed outbreaks were identified, comprising 13 genotype B3 outbreaks and 17 genotype D8 outbreaks. The discriminatory power and concordance with epidemiological data in the context of outbreak investigations was substantially greater for WGS data than for conventional N450-based genotyping. Viruses sharing the same N450-based distinct sequence identifier (DSid) differed by up to 33 single nucleotide polymorphisms (SNPs) for genotype B3 and 36 SNPs for genotype D8, whereas genomes obtained from epidemiologically linked cases differed by a maximum of four SNPs. Despite increased national case numbers in 2025, available molecular and epidemiological evidence indicated that cases were associated with importations or import-related transmission events, with no evidence of sustained endemic measles transmission. Rubella activity remained extremely low, with no rubella virus detections identified among 157 specimens tested by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) and only two notified cases nationally, neither of which had material available for genotyping. These findings support the continued absence of endemic measles and rubella transmission in Australia and highlight the value of WGS for enhancing measles surveillance and outbreak investigations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Molecular characterisation is a critical component of Australia's measles and rubella surveillance systems, supporting outbreak investigations, identifying sources of infection, and providing evidence for the continued maintenance of Australia's measles and rubella elimination status.
Design and population
This annual report summarises measles and rubella molecular epidemiology in Australia during 2025 and evaluates the utility of whole genome sequencing (WGS) for measles surveillance.
Core methods
In 2025, there were 181 confirmed measles cases notified in Australia, representing a 217.5% increase on the number notified in 2024. Rubella activity remained extremely low, with no rubella virus detections identified among 157 specimens tested by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) and only two notified cases nationally, neither of which had material available for genotyping.
Main results
Measles virus genotypes B3 and D8 were detected, together with 21 vaccine-associated genotype A cases. Thirty-one epidemiologically confirmed outbreaks were identified, comprising 13 genotype B3 outbreaks and 17 genotype D8 outbreaks.
Interpretation and novelty
Genotype B3 predominated during the first half of the year and was largely associated with importations from Vietnam, whereas genotype D8 predominated during the second half of the year and was primarily associated with importations from Indonesia.
Scientific and public-health significance
The discriminatory power and concordance with epidemiological data in the context of outbreak investigations was substantially greater for WGS data than for conventional N450-based genotyping. These findings support the continued absence of endemic measles and rubella transmission in Australia and highlight the value of WGS for enhancing measles surveillance and outbreak investigations.
Limitations and evidence strength
Viruses sharing the same N450-based distinct sequence identifier (DSid) differed by up to 33 single nucleotide polymorphisms (SNPs) for genotype B3 and 36 SNPs for genotype D8, whereas genomes obtained from epidemiologically linked cases differed by a maximum of four SNPs.

美国孕妇及新生儿麻疹结局的初步分析,2025-2026年。

Outcomes among Pregnant Women with Measles and Their Infants in the United States - Preliminary Analysis, 2025-2026.
作者： Elizabeth L. Lewis, Daniel Chang, Nicole G. Wiley, Nicole M. Roth, Chinyere Ezekwe, Lois A. Park, Van T. Tong, Carla L. DeSisto, Lily Lee, Ruth Link-Gelles
摘要中文翻译摘要:妊娠期间麻疹病毒感染可能导致严重的母体疾病、不良的妊娠和婴儿结局以及新生儿并发症,包括先天性麻疹。在2025年1月1日至2026年9月28日期间发生的多次大型暴发中,向疾病控制与预防中心报告了112例妊娠期间确诊的麻疹病例和13例先天性麻疹病例;并发症包括母体肺炎、早产和新生儿并发症。几乎所有患有麻疹的孕妇(98%)在怀孕前未接种麻疹-麻疹-腮腺炎(MMR)疫苗或其疫苗接种状态未知。在怀孕前接种MMR疫苗以及社区中高MMR疫苗接种覆盖率对于预防麻疹及其相关并发症至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解2025年1月1日至2026年9月28日期间多次大型暴发中,患有麻疹的孕妇及其婴儿的结局,鉴于已知麻疹在妊娠期间与严重的母体和新生儿并发症相关。
设计与对象
本研究涉及向疾病控制与预防中心报告的112例妊娠期间确诊的麻疹病例和13例先天性麻疹病例,其并发症包括母体肺炎、早产和新生儿并发症。
核心方法
本研究分析了报告病例的数据,关注母体和新生儿结局,并强调了在怀孕前接种MMR疫苗对于预防麻疹及其相关并发症的重要性。
主要结果
几乎所有患有麻疹的孕妇(98%)在怀孕前未接种MMR疫苗或其疫苗接种状态未知,研究发现,在怀孕前接种MMR疫苗以及社区中高MMR疫苗接种覆盖率对于预防麻疹及其相关并发症至关重要。
解释与创新
作者解释了这些发现,强调在怀孕前接种MMR疫苗对于预防麻疹及其并发症的关键作用,并突出了提高疫苗接种覆盖率的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为公共卫生策略提供信息,以提高疫苗接种覆盖率,从而预防妊娠期间麻疹病毒及其相关并发症对母体和新生儿的影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究受限于对报告病例的依赖以及缺乏详细的干预方案,但证据表明在怀孕前接种MMR 疫苗接种的重要性。

Outcomes among Pregnant Women with Measles and Their Infants in the United States - Preliminary Analysis, 2025-2026.

Authors: Elizabeth L. Lewis, Daniel Chang, Nicole G. Wiley, Nicole M. Roth, Chinyere Ezekwe, Lois A. Park, Van T. Tong, Carla L. DeSisto, Lily Lee, Ruth Link-Gelles
Original abstract (English)
AbstractMeasles virus infection during pregnancy can result in severe maternal illness, adverse pregnancy and infant outcomes, and neonatal complications, including congenital measles. During multiple large outbreaks from January 1, 2025, to September 28, 2026, a total of 112 cases of confirmed measles during pregnancy and 13 cases of congenital measles were reported to the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention; complications included maternal pneumonia, preterm delivery, and neonatal complications. Almost all pregnant women with measles (98%) had not received measles-mumps-rubella (MMR) vaccination before pregnancy or had unknown vaccination status. MMR vaccination before pregnancy and high community MMR vaccination coverage are important to prevent measles and related complications.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the outcomes of pregnant women with measles and their infants during multiple large outbreaks from January 1, 2025, to September 28, 2026, given the known severe maternal and neonatal complications associated with measles during pregnancy.
Design and population
The study involved 112 cases of confirmed measles during pregnancy and 13 cases of congenital measles reported to the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention, with complications including maternal pneumonia, preterm delivery, and neonatal complications.
Core methods
The study analyzed data from reported cases, focusing on maternal and neonatal outcomes, and highlighted the importance of MMR vaccination before pregnancy to prevent measles and related complications.
Main results
Almost all pregnant women with measles (98%) had not received MMR vaccination before pregnancy or had unknown vaccination status, and MMR vaccination before pregnancy and high community MMR vaccination coverage were found to be important to prevent measles and related complications.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings to emphasize the critical role of MMR vaccination before pregnancy in preventing measles and its complications, highlighting the need for improved vaccination coverage.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health strategies to improve vaccination coverage and prevent maternal and neonatal complications from measles during pregnancy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the reliance on reported cases and the lack of detailed intervention protocols, but the evidence is strong for the importance of MMR vaccination before pregnancy.

Journal: Arts & health  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-16  |  DOI: 10.1080/17533015.2026.2752310  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 1-28
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

日本麻疹 治疗历史的进化通过日本艺术作品和木版画可视化呈现。

The historical evolution of measles treatment in Japan visualised through Japanese artwork and woodblock prints.
作者： Jo Ern Tay, Yu Yue Hew, Hanna Osawa, Sharavan Sadasiv Mucheli, Bingwen Eugene Fan
摘要中文翻译背景:麻疹疫情在日本持续数个世纪。在近代之前,管理措施借鉴了汉方医学、民间疗法、宗教仪式和护身符习俗。视觉文化常常在医学知识与公众之间起到中介作用,尤其是在1862年麻疹 暴发疫情等危机期间,以及后来明治时代向现代科学医学过渡的时期。方法:我们采用跨学科的医学人文研究方法,结合历史研究和定性视觉分析。对一组精选的日本视觉材料(包括汉方医学插图、hashika-e(麻疹木版画)以及早期隔离和防控的描绘)进行了分析,以确定图像和相关文字如何传达症状识别、治疗方案、防护措施和面向公众的指导。解释基于同时代的文献和相关次级学术研究。结果:艺术媒介将复杂的医学概念转化为可操作的家庭建议,通常结合治疗指导(特别是饮食和支持性护理)与精神和护身符保护。在疫情时期,尤其是1862年,hashika-e作为大众传播的公共卫生信息,塑造了权威形象,动员了普通民众的参与,并在某些情况下表达了讽刺或道德批评。在不同时期,视觉记录显示了从以精神解释框架为主的模式逐渐向现代科学治理转变,包括防控措施和以疫苗接种为导向的预防。结论:日本的艺术作品和印刷品提供了独特的视角,审视麻疹管理、文化与信任之间的相互作用。日本最终消除麻疹可以被视为一项社会技术成就,其中生物医学进展得到了与文化相契合的视觉策略的支持,从而增强了公众信心和合作。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹疫情在日本持续数个世纪,本研究旨在探讨历史视觉文化如何在这些时期中介医学知识与公众理解。
设计与对象
本研究是一项跨学科的医学人文分析,研究对象包括日本的视觉材料,如汉方医学插图、hashika-e木版画以及早期隔离措施的描绘,重点聚焦于1862年麻疹 暴发疫情和明治时代向现代医学过渡的时期。
核心方法
本研究采用结合历史研究和定性视觉分析的跨学科方法,解释基于同时代的文献和次级学术研究。
主要结果
艺术媒介将复杂的医学概念转化为可操作的家庭建议,通常结合治疗指导(特别是饮食和支持性护理)与精神和护身符保护。
解释与创新
视觉记录显示了一种从精神解释框架向现代科学治理的逐渐转变,包括防控措施和以疫苗接种为导向的预防,为审视麻疹管理与公众信任之间的相互作用提供了独特的视角。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果提供了关于视觉策略如何增强公众对健康措施的信心和合作的见解,对理解历史公共卫生传播及其对当代疾病预防的相关性具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于历史视觉材料和次级学术研究,可能引入解释性偏见并限制因果推论的强度,尽管证据是在其历史框架内进行语境化的。

The historical evolution of measles treatment in Japan visualised through Japanese artwork and woodblock prints.

Authors: Jo Ern Tay, Yu Yue Hew, Hanna Osawa, Sharavan Sadasiv Mucheli, Bingwen Eugene Fan
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Measles epidemics recurred for centuries in Japan. In the pre-modern era, management drew on kampō (Chinese-influenced) medicine, folk remedies, religious rituals, and talismanic practices. Visual culture often mediated between medical knowledge and the public, especially during crises such as the 1862 measles outbreak and later during the Meiji-era transition toward modern scientific medicine. METHODS: We conducted an interdisciplinary medical humanities study using historical inquiry and qualitative visual analysis. A curated corpus of Japanese visual materials - including kampō medical illustrations, hashika-e (measles woodblock prints), and depictions of early quarantine and containment - was analysed to determine how images and accompanying texts communicated symptom recognition, therapeutic regimens, protective practices, and public-facing guidance. Interpretations were contextualised using contemporaneous writings and relevant secondary scholarship. RESULTS: Artistic media translated complex medical ideas into actionable household advice, commonly combining regimen guidance (notably diet and supportive care) with spiritual and talismanic protection. During epidemic periods, particularly 1862, hashika-e functioned as mass-circulating public health messaging, shaping perceptions of authority, mobilising lay participation, and at times expressing satire or moral critique. Across eras, the visual record tracks a gradual paradigm shift from predominantly spiritual explanatory frameworks toward modern scientific governance, including containment practices and vaccination-oriented prevention. CONCLUSION: Japanese artworks and printed ephemera offer a distinctive lens on the co-evolution of measles management, culture, and trust. Japan's eventual measles elimination can be understood as a socio-technical achievement in which biomedical advances were reinforced by culturally resonant visual strategies that supported public confidence and cooperation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles epidemics recurred for centuries in Japan, and the study aimed to explore how historical visual culture mediated medical knowledge and public understanding during these periods.
Design and population
The study was an interdisciplinary medical humanities analysis of Japanese visual materials, including kampō medical illustrations, hashika-e woodblock prints, and depictions of early quarantine practices, focusing on the 1862 measles outbreak and the Meiji-era transition to modern medicine.
Core methods

Main results
Artistic media translated complex medical ideas into actionable household advice, commonly combining regimen guidance (notably diet and supportive care) with spiritual and talismanic protection.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into how visual strategies can reinforce public confidence and cooperation in health measures, with implications for understanding historical public health communication and its relevance to contemporary disease prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on historical visual materials and secondary scholarship, which may introduce interpretive bias and limit the strength of causal inferences, though the evidence is contextualised within its historical framework.

十四年来西西里岛的监测疫情:流行病学趋势、成人易感性及年龄特异性临床特征

Fourteen years of measles surveillance in Sicily: epidemiological trends, adult susceptibility, and age-specific clinical profiles.
作者： Fabio Tramuto, Giulia Randazzo, Rita Li Muli, Giuseppe Sferlazza, Valeria Guzzetta, Arianna Santino, Adriana Previti, Carmelo Massimo Maida
摘要中文翻译背景:麻疹仍然是全球主要的公共卫生问题,包括在高收入国家和欧洲地区,这些地区疫苗接种覆盖率不足,无法中断传播传播。我们分析了长期的监测数据,以描述西西里岛14年期间的麻疹疫情及临床特征。方法:分析了2012年7月至2026年5月期间报告的实验室确诊病例,按人口统计学特征、疫苗接种状态、医疗环境、症状、并发症和时间进行分类。使用描述性统计、单变量逻辑回归和phi系数评估年龄相关关联和症状共现情况。结果:在1,169例病例中,2018年出现最大疫情波(598例;51.1%)。在2020年至2023年期间未报告传播,但2024年传播重新出现。大多数患者未接种疫苗(93.5%);成人病例占60.7%,其中包括46名医护人员(成人病例的6.5%)。约四分之三的病例由医院管理,其中51.8%出现临床并发症。在社区管理病例中,成人出现并发症的风险高于儿童(OR 2.89),在医院管理病例中,成人出现并发症的风险也高于儿童(OR 1.94)。呼吸道黏膜模式占48.0%,在医院管理患者中更为常见(51.1% vs 38.4%)。淋巴关节模式罕见(3.2%),且仅见于成人。结论:疫情重新出现反映了持续的免疫缺口。研究结果支持儿童高覆盖率的两剂疫苗接种、青少年和成人补种疫苗接种以及系统性的医护人员免疫评估。这些发现突显了各年龄段持续的易感性,并强调了需要实验室支持的监测监测和有效的针对性消除策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在描述西西里岛14年期间的麻疹疫情,应对麻疹这一持续的公共卫生问题,特别是在高收入地区疫苗接种覆盖率不足的情况下。
设计与对象
本研究分析了2012年7月至2026年5月期间在西西里岛报告的实验室确诊麻疹病例,共涉及1,169例病例,涵盖各种人口统计学特征和医疗环境。
核心方法
病例通过描述性统计、单变量逻辑回归和phi系数进行分析,以评估年龄相关关联和症状共现情况。
主要结果
病例最大波峰出现在2018年(598例),大多数患者未接种疫苗(93.5%),成人病例占60.7%,其中包括46名医护人员。医院管理的病例出现并发症的风险更高,且呼吸道黏膜模式在医院管理患者中更为常见。
解释与创新
研究结果突出了麻疹的重新出现,并强调了需要儿童高覆盖率的两剂疫苗接种、青少年和成人补种疫苗接种以及系统性的医护人员免疫评估。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现突显了各年龄段持续的易感性,并强调了需要实验室支持的监测监测和针对性消除策略。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性包括缺乏随机化以及由于数据为观察性而可能存在的偏倚,但所描述的趋势和关联的证据是强有力的。

Fourteen years of measles surveillance in Sicily: epidemiological trends, adult susceptibility, and age-specific clinical profiles.

Authors: Fabio Tramuto, Giulia Randazzo, Rita Li Muli, Giuseppe Sferlazza, Valeria Guzzetta, Arianna Santino, Adriana Previti, Carmelo Massimo Maida
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Measles remains a major public health concern globally, including in high-income countries and European regions where vaccination coverage is insufficient to interrupt transmission. We analysed long-term surveillance data to describe measles epidemiology in Sicily and clinical profiles over a 14-year period. METHODS: Laboratory-confirmed cases reported from July 2012 to May 2026 were analysed by demographics, vaccination status, healthcare setting, symptoms, complications, and time. Descriptive tests, univariate logistic regression, and phi coefficients assessed age-related associations and symptom co-occurrence. RESULTS: Among 1,169 cases, the largest wave occurred in 2018 (598; 51.1%). After no reported circulation during 2020-2023, transmission re-emerged in 2024. Most patients were unvaccinated (93.5%); adults accounted for 60.7% of cases, including 46 healthcare workers (6.5% of adult cases). Approximately threee-quarters of cases were hospital-managed and, among these, 51.8% showed clinical complications. Adults had higher odds of complications than children in community-managed (OR 2.89) and hospital-managed cases (OR 1.94). The respiratory-mucosal pattern occurred in 48.0% and was more frequent in hospital-managed patients (51.1% vs 38.4%). The lympho-articular pattern was rare (3.2%) and adult-specific. CONCLUSION: Re-emergence reflects persistent immunity gaps. Findings support high two-dose childhood coverage, catch-up vaccination for adolescents and adults, and systematic healthcare-worker immunity assessment. These findings highlight persistent susceptibility across age groups and the need for laboratory-supported surveillance and effective targeted elimination strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to describe measles epidemiology in Sicily over a 14-year period, addressing the ongoing public health concern of measles, particularly in high-income regions with insufficient vaccination coverage.
Design and population
The study analysed laboratory-confirmed measles cases reported from July 2012 to May 2026 in Sicily, involving 1,169 cases across various demographics and healthcare settings.
Core methods
Cases were analysed using descriptive tests, univariate logistic regression, and phi coefficients to assess age-related associations and symptom co-occurrence.
Main results
The largest wave of cases occurred in 2018 (598 cases), with most patients being unvaccinated (93.5%) and adults accounting for 60.7% of cases, including 46 healthcare workers. Hospital-managed cases showed higher odds of complications, and the respiratory-mucosal pattern was more frequent in hospital-managed patients.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the re-emergence of measles and the need for high two-dose childhood coverage, catch-up vaccination for adolescents and adults, and systematic healthcare-worker immunity assessment.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings underscore the persistent susceptibility across age groups and the necessity for laboratory-supported surveillance and targeted elimination strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the absence of randomization and potential bias due to the observational nature of the data, but the evidence is strong for the described trends and associations.

具有不完整 疫苗接种 和 1 个免疫力减弱的 麻疹 的 SVIR 模型分析:Sidoarjo-Indonesia 案例

SVIR Model Analysis of Measles with Incomplete Vaccination and 1 Waning Immunity: Sidoarjo-Indonesia Case
作者： Ainishafa Ainishafa, Abadi Abadi, Dian Savitri
摘要中文翻译麻疹仍然是印度尼西亚的重大公共卫生挑战,尤其是在疫苗接种覆盖率异质性和免疫力下降的地区。本研究开发并分析了一个针对麻疹传播的易感-接种-感染-康复(SVIR)分组模型,该模型纳入了免疫力下降的因素。基本再生数R0是通过下一代矩阵方法推导的,并且通过李雅普诺夫函数严格证明了无病平衡态和地方性平衡态的全局稳定性。模型参数是从2023年Sidoarjo市每周的监测数据中使用粒子群优化(PSO)、扩展卡尔曼滤波、最大似然估计和最小二乘法进行估计,并通过平均绝对百分比误差(MAPE)进行评估。PSO方法实现了最低的MAPE(11.17%),优于其他方法。估计的R0 = 0.668确认了无病状态。归一化敏感性分析确定疫苗效力是最关键的参数;疫苗效力下降至0 .75会使R0接近临界阈值,任何进一步的减少都会使系统进入地方性状态。这些发现强调了通过加强剂接种计划和强化疫苗接种策略保持高疫苗效力的必要性,以在区级维持麻疹的消除。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
使用下一代矩阵方法推导出基本繁殖数R0,并通过Lyapunov函数严格证明了无病平衡和地方病平衡的全局稳定性。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
本研究开发并分析了印度尼西亚诗都阿佐市麻疹 传播 的易感-疫苗接种-感染-康复 (SVIR) 区室模型,其中考虑了免疫力的减弱。
主要结果
模型参数是根据西多阿佐市 2023 年每周的 监测 数据使用粒子群优化 (PSO)、扩展卡尔曼滤波器、最大似然估计和最小二乘法进行估计,并通过平均绝对百分比误差 (MAPE) 进行评估。PSO 实现了最低的 MAPE (11.17%),优于其他方法。
解释与创新
估计的 R0 = 0.668 确认无病状态。
科研与公卫意义
麻疹 在印度尼西亚仍然是一个重大的公共卫生挑战,特别是在 麻疹 覆盖范围不均匀且免疫力下降的地区。
局限与证据强度
Normalized sensitivity analysis identifies vaccineefficacy as the most influential parameter; a decline in efficacy to0 .75elevates R0 close tothe critical threshold, and any further reduction pushes the system into an endemic state.These findings underscore the necessity of maintaining high vaccine efficacy through boosterprograms and strengthened immunization strategies to sustain measles elimination at thedistrict level.

SVIR Model Analysis of Measles with Incomplete Vaccination and 1 Waning Immunity: Sidoarjo-Indonesia Case

Authors: Ainishafa Ainishafa, Abadi Abadi, Dian Savitri
Original abstract (English)
Measles remains a significant public health challenge in Indonesia, especially in regionswith heterogeneous vaccination coverage and waning immunity. This study develops andanalyzes a Susceptible–Vaccinated–Infected–Recovered (SVIR) compartmental model formeasles transmission in Sidoarjo City, Indonesia, incorporating waning immunity. The basicreproduction number R0 is derived using the next-generation matrix method, and the globalstability of both the disease-free and endemic equilibria is rigorously proven via Lyapunovfunctions. Model parameters are estimated from weekly 2023 surveillance data in Sidoarjo Cityusing Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO), Extended Kalman Filter, Maximum LikelihoodEstimation, and Least Squares, and evaluated via Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE).PSO achieves the lowest MAPE (11.17%), outperforming the other methods. The estimatedR0 = 0.668confirms a disease-free regime. Normalized sensitivity analysis identifies vaccineefficacy as the most influential parameter; a decline in efficacy to0 .75elevates R0 close tothe critical threshold, and any further reduction pushes the system into an endemic state.These findings underscore the necessity of maintaining high vaccine efficacy through boosterprograms and strengthened immunization strategies to sustain measles elimination at thedistrict level.

The basicreproduction number R0 is derived using the next-generation matrix method, and the globalstability of both the disease-free and endemic equilibria is rigorously proven via Lyapunovfunctions.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.
Core methods
This study develops andanalyzes a Susceptible–Vaccinated–Infected–Recovered (SVIR) compartmental model formeasles transmission in Sidoarjo City, Indonesia, incorporating waning immunity.
Main results
Model parameters are estimated from weekly 2023 surveillance data in Sidoarjo Cityusing Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO), Extended Kalman Filter, Maximum LikelihoodEstimation, and Least Squares, and evaluated via Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE).PSO achieves the lowest MAPE (11.17%), outperforming the other methods.
Interpretation and novelty
The estimatedR0 = 0.668confirms a disease-free regime.
Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

高覆盖率环境下麻疹簇暴发的延迟识别:河南省2025年不同免疫状态的首发病例和指数病例的流行病学与临床特征

Delayed Recognition of Measles Cluster Outbreaks in a High-Vaccination-Coverage Setting: Epidemiological and Clinical Characteristics of Primary and Index Cases with Different Immune Statuses, Henan Province, China, 2025
作者： Jing Li, Binghui Du, Zheng Tian, Xiaoli Wang, Xiaoxiao Zhang, Yonghao Guo, Yanyang Zhang
摘要中文翻译背景/目的:在高疫苗接种覆盖率下,突破性感染常表现出轻微、非典型的症状,这可能会延迟对感染的识别。我们对高覆盖率环境下麻疹病毒(麻疹病毒)的暴发进行了特征分析,重点关注首发病例与索引病例发病时间间隔作为延迟识别的衡量指标。方法:我们通过现场调查和实验室检测,对河南省2025年报告的66例实验室确诊的麻疹病毒暴发病例进行了研究,并比较了不同疫苗接种状态下的临床表现;由于疫苗接种组之间存在显著的年龄差异,比较结果以描述性方式呈现。结果:首发病例到索引病例发病的时间间隔为4至15天,代表了识别延迟的上限估计。所有三个首发病例均接种了两剂含麻疹成分的疫苗(MCV),并表现出低热和局部皮疹,而四个索引病例均无MCV接种史,且最高体温均≥39 °C。在100名密切接触者中筛查出21名感染病例,其中13名接种过疫苗(≥1剂)的密切接触者中有84.6%(11/13)为无症状或轻症,而8名未接种或接种状态未知的密切接触者中有37.5%(3/8)为无症状或轻症。在有明确疫苗接种史的病例中,接种疫苗者比未接种者肺炎发生率(17.4% vs. 37.5%)和住院率(43.5% vs. 62.5%)均较低;这些未调整的差异受到年龄的影响,因此以描述性方式呈现。结论:在高覆盖率环境下,突破性麻疹病毒感染表现出非典型症状和轻微症状。此类感染可能导致社区中未被识别的传播。在高覆盖率和高密度环境下,监测系统应纳入对非典型表现的主动病例发现。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景/目标:在高麻疹 疫苗接种覆盖率环境下,突破性感染通常表现为轻度、非典型的症状,可能延迟暴发的识别。
设计与对象
我们研究了高覆盖率环境下三个麻疹簇暴发,共包含66例实验室确诊病例,重点关注首发病例与指数病例发病间隔作为延迟暴发识别的衡量指标。
核心方法
我们通过现场调查和实验室检测,研究了2025年河南省报告的三个麻疹簇暴发,共66例实验室确诊病例,并根据疫苗接种状态比较临床表现;由于疫苗接种组之间存在显著的年龄差异,比较结果以描述性方式呈现。
主要结果
首发病例与指数病例发病间隔为4至15天,代表了识别延迟的上限估计。所有三个首发病例均接种了两剂含麻疹的疫苗(MCV),表现为低热和局部皮疹,而四个指数病例均无MCV接种史,且所有病例的最高体温均≥39 °C。在有明确疫苗接种史的病例中,接种者比未接种者或状态未知者出现肺炎(17.4% vs. 37.5%)和住院(43.5% vs. 62.5%)的频率较低;这些未调整的差异受到年龄因素的干扰,以描述性方式呈现。
解释与创新
在高覆盖率环境下,突破性麻疹感染表现出非典型症状和轻度症状。此类感染可能导致未被识别的社区传播。
科研与公卫意义
在高覆盖率高密度环境中,监测应纳入对非典型表现的主动病例发现。
局限与证据强度
未调整的差异受到年龄因素的干扰,且本研究仅聚焦于三个暴发,因此其推广性有限。

Delayed Recognition of Measles Cluster Outbreaks in a High-Vaccination-Coverage Setting: Epidemiological and Clinical Characteristics of Primary and Index Cases with Different Immune Statuses, Henan Province, China, 2025

Authors: Jing Li, Binghui Du, Zheng Tian, Xiaoli Wang, Xiaoxiao Zhang, Yonghao Guo, Yanyang Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: Under high measles vaccination coverage, breakthrough infections often present with mild, atypical features that may delay outbreak recognition. We characterized measles cluster outbreaks in a high-coverage setting, focusing on the interval between primary-case and index-case onset as a measure of delayed outbreak recognition. Methods: We investigated three measles cluster outbreaks comprising 66 laboratory-confirmed cases reported in Henan Province, China, in 2025, using field investigation and laboratory testing, and compared clinical manifestations by immunization status; because of profound age differences between immunization groups, comparisons were presented descriptively. Results: The interval from primary- to index-case onset ranged from 4 to 15 days, representing an upper-bound estimate of the recognition delay. All three primary cases had received two doses of measles-containing vaccine (MCV) and presented with low-grade fever and localized rash, whereas none of the four index cases had an MCV history and all had peak temperatures ≥39 °C. Among the 21 infected close contacts identified from 100 screened close contacts, 84.6% (11/13) of vaccinated (≥1 dose) individuals were asymptomatic or mild, versus 37.5% (3/8) of unvaccinated or status-unknown individuals. Among cases with documented immunization history, pneumonia (17.4% vs. 37.5%) and hospitalization (43.5% vs. 62.5%) were less frequent in vaccinated than in unvaccinated cases; these unadjusted differences were confounded by age and are presented descriptively. Conclusions: Breakthrough measles infections in high-coverage settings have atypical presentation and mild symptoms. Such infections may lead to unrecognized community transmission. Surveillance should incorporate active case finding for atypical presentations in high-coverage high-density settings.
View research seven-element analysis

关于疫苗接种阈值的最新更新以用于麻疹群体免疫

An update to the critical vaccination threshold for measles herd immunity
作者： Mahmuda Ruma, Elena Aruffo, Alexandra Teslya, Jane M. Heffernan
摘要中文翻译摘要 近期的证据表明,疫苗诱导的免疫力可能会随时间减弱,这挑战了传统上认为免疫力终身存在的假设。我们开发了一个关于麻疹 传播和疫苗接种的数学模型,该模型明确纳入了免疫力减弱的因素,并推导出更新后的疫苗接种阈值($$p_c$$)和控制再生数($$R_c$$)的表达式,这两个指标是指导公共卫生疫苗接种政策的关键参数。我们还推导了暴发概率和最终暴发规模的方程。我们将该模型应用于2024-2025年麻疹 暴发在西南安大略公共卫生地区的情况,并报告了符合观察到的疫情特征的可行参数集所对应的基线再生数($$R_0$$)范围、暴发峰值时间与规模、最终暴发规模以及参与传播的人口规模。最重要的是,我们的分析表明,即使疫苗诱导的免疫力减弱极其缓慢——实际上相当于终身保护——也可能从根本上改变群体免疫的动态。具体而言,在模型假设下,我们识别出一些现实的参数范围,在这些范围内,尽管疫苗接种率很高,但无法实现疫苗诱导的群体免疫。然而,在这些条件下,暴发的风险可能仍然非常低。这些发现表明,在高疫苗接种率人群中,广泛使用的麻疹群体免疫阈值可能需要重新评估。

An update to the critical vaccination threshold for measles herd immunity

Authors: Mahmuda Ruma, Elena Aruffo, Alexandra Teslya, Jane M. Heffernan
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Recent evidence indicates that measles vaccine-induced immunity may wane over time, challenging the classical assumption of lifelong protection. We develop a mathematical model of measles transmission and vaccination that explicitly incorporates waning immunity and derive updated expressions for the critical vaccination threshold ( $$p_c$$ ) and control reproduction number ( $$R_c$$ ), two cornerstone metrics used to guide public health vaccination policy. We additionally derive equations for outbreak probability and final outbreak size. We apply the model to the 2024-2025 measles outbreak in the Southwestern Ontario Public Health Region and report ranges of the basic reproduction number ( $$R_0$$ ), outbreak peak timing and magnitude, final outbreak size, and the size of the population involved in transmission from feasible parameter sets that satisfy observed epidemic characteristics. Most importantly, our analysis reveals that even extremely slow waning of vaccine-induced immunity – effectively corresponding to lifelong protection – can fundamentally alter herd immunity dynamics. Specifically, under the model assumptions, we identify realistic parameter regimes in which vaccine-induced herd immunity cannot be achieved, despite high vaccine coverage. Nevertheless, outbreak risk under these conditions may still remain very low. These findings suggest that widely used measles herd immunity thresholds may require re-evaluation in highly vaccinated populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Recent evidence indicates that measles vaccine-induced immunity may wane over time, challenging the classical assumption of lifelong protection.
Design and population
We develop a mathematical model of measles transmission and vaccination that explicitly incorporates waning immunity and derive updated expressions for the critical vaccination threshold ( $$p_c$$ ) and control reproduction number ( $$R_c$$ ), two cornerstone metrics used to guide public health vaccination policy.
Core methods
We additionally derive equations for outbreak probability and final outbreak size.
Main results
We apply the model to the 2024-2025 measles outbreak in the Southwestern Ontario Public Health Region and report ranges of the basic reproduction number ( $$R_0$$ ), outbreak peak timing and magnitude, final outbreak size, and the size of the population involved in transmission from feasible parameter sets that satisfy observed epidemic characteristics.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that widely used measles herd immunity thresholds may require re-evaluation in highly vaccinated populations.
Scientific and public-health significance
Specifically, under the model assumptions, we identify realistic parameter regimes in which vaccine-induced herd immunity cannot be achieved, despite high vaccine coverage.
Limitations and evidence strength
Nevertheless, outbreak risk under these conditions may still remain very low.

在2024年芝加哥麻疹期间无皮疹人群麻疹实时逆转录聚合酶链反应检测的敏感性

Sensitivity of Measles Real-time Reverse-transcription Polymerase Chain Reaction Testing During an Outbreak in Persons Without Rash.
作者： Kimberly Gressick, Stephanie Gretsch, Thomas D Filardo, Peter M DeJonge, Brian F Borah
摘要中文翻译在2024年芝加哥麻疹 暴发期间,对117名无皮疹但有麻疹暴露的有症状人群进行了120次实时逆转录聚合酶链反应(RT-PCR)检测,检测敏感性为78%(7例真实阳性)。在初次检测结果为阴性后,医疗提供者应继续怀疑麻疹,并在症状进展时重复检测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估在暴发期间,无皮疹但暴露于麻疹的个体中,麻疹实时逆转录聚合酶链反应(RT-PCR)检测的敏感性,以应对在缺乏特征性皮疹的情况下诊断麻疹的挑战。
设计与对象
本研究在2024年芝加哥麻疹 暴发期间进行,涉及117名无皮疹但有麻疹暴露的有症状人群,共进行了120次RT-PCR检测。
核心方法
使用实时逆转录聚合酶链反应(RT-PCR)检测无皮疹有症状个体的标本中麻疹病毒的RNA,若初次检测结果为阴性,则重复检测。
主要结果
麻疹 RT-PCR检测的敏感性报告为78%,在120次检测中获得了7例真实阳性结果。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了在初次检测结果为阴性时重复检测的重要性,因为麻疹仍可能存在于无皮疹的个体中,这对暴发期间的准确诊断具有重要意义。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为非皮疹情况下麻疹的诊断协议提供信息,从而提高监测和在暴发期间的早期检测能力。
局限与证据强度
由于本研究具有观察性质且存在假阴性风险,因此存在一定的局限性;然而,由于在真实世界暴发环境中直接观察了检测结果,证据强度较强。

Sensitivity of Measles Real-time Reverse-transcription Polymerase Chain Reaction Testing During an Outbreak in Persons Without Rash.

Authors: Kimberly Gressick, Stephanie Gretsch, Thomas D Filardo, Peter M DeJonge, Brian F Borah
Original abstract (English)
During the 2024 Chicago measles outbreak, 120 real-time reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction tests were performed on 117 symptomatic persons with measles exposure, but without rash; test sensitivity was 78% (7 true positive). After an initial negative test, providers should maintain suspicion of measles and repeat testing as symptoms progress.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the sensitivity of measles real-time reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) testing in individuals exposed to measles but without rash during an outbreak, addressing the challenge of diagnosing measles in the absence of the characteristic rash.
Design and population
The study was conducted during the 2024 Chicago measles outbreak and involved 117 symptomatic persons with measles exposure but without rash, with 120 RT-PCR tests performed on them.
Core methods
Real-time reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) testing was used to detect measles virus RNA in specimens from symptomatic individuals without rash, with testing repeated if initial results were negative.
Main results
The sensitivity of measles RT-PCR testing was reported as 78%, with 7 true positive results out of 120 tests performed.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the importance of repeated testing in cases where initial results are negative, as measles can still be present in individuals without rash, which has implications for accurate diagnosis during outbreaks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform diagnostic protocols for measles in non-rash cases, improving surveillance and early detection during outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational nature and potential for false negatives, but the evidence is strong due to the direct observation of test results in a real-world outbreak setting.

描述麻疹医疗机构暴露情况:2023年12月至2025年9月:纽约市。

Characterizing Measles Healthcare Facility Exposures-December 2023 Through September 2025: New York City.
作者： Melanie S Askari, Robert J Arciuolo, Krishika A Graham, Beth M Isaac, Antonine Jean, Jie Fu, Hannah Gray, Jennifer B Rosen
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在总结在纽约市(NYC)医疗机构中接触麻疹病例的接触者的暴露情况和传播,以应对常见的医疗机构暴露和资源密集型的控制措施需求。
设计与对象
一项回顾性描述性研究分析了纽约市卫生部门在2023年12月至2025年9月期间,所有在纽约市医疗机构中接触麻疹病例的接触者的监测数据。
核心方法
本研究利用纽约市卫生部门的监测数据,分析接触麻疹病例的接触者特征,包括麻疹暴露时间、免疫状态、暴露后预防(PEP)措施、机构环境以及暴露时间。
主要结果
在28例病例的2122名医疗接触者中,未发生二次病例。1285名(60.6%)已接种疫苗或有其他免疫证据,618名(29.1%)为免疫状态未知的成年人,124名(5.8%)无免疫力且接受了暴露后预防(PEP),95名(4.5%)无免疫力且未接受PEP。大多数接触者是在急诊科和/或医院住院环境中暴露的(1406名;66.3%)。
解释与创新
本研究发现,大多数在纽约市医疗机构中接触麻疹的接触者具有推测性免疫或为免疫状态未知的成年人,并且在2122名接触者中未发现任何传播病例,包括无免疫力的接触者。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,仅依赖基于时间的麻疹暴露定义可能过于宽泛,而结合策略如医疗机构空气交换数据,可能更准确地评估传播风险。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其回顾性设计以及可能存在未测量的混杂因素。证据强度为中等。

Characterizing Measles Healthcare Facility Exposures-December 2023 Through September 2025: New York City.

Authors: Melanie S Askari, Robert J Arciuolo, Krishika A Graham, Beth M Isaac, Antonine Jean, Jie Fu, Hannah Gray, Jennifer B Rosen
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: When people develop measles (cases), healthcare facility exposures are common and result in many potentially exposed people requiring resource-intensive control measures. We summarized exposures and transmission among people exposed to measles (contacts) in healthcare settings in New York City (NYC). METHODS: NYC Health Department's surveillance data of NYC contacts exposed to measles cases in any NYC healthcare facility during December 2023-September 2025 were analyzed as a retrospective descriptive study. Characteristics summarized included measles transmission, immunity status, postexposure prophylaxis (PEP) administration, facility setting, and time of measles exposure. RESULTS: Of 2122 healthcare contacts to 28 cases, no secondary cases occurred. 1285 (60.6%) were vaccinated or had other evidence of immunity, 618 (29.1%) were adults with unknown immunity, 124 (5.8%) were not immune and received PEP, and 95 (4.5%) were not immune and did not receive PEP. Most contacts were exposed in emergency departments and/or hospital inpatient settings (1406; 66.3%). Among 1028 contacts with known time of exposure (48.4% of all contacts), 785 (76.4%) overlapped with the case in the healthcare facility, 146 (14.2%) arrived within 1 hour, and 97 (9.4%) arrived 1-2 hours after the case left or were isolated. CONCLUSIONS: Most contacts exposed to measles in NYC healthcare facilities had presumptive immunity or were adults with unknown immunity. No transmission occurred among 2122 exposed contacts, including nonimmune contacts. Given no secondary transmission and resource-intensive control measures, relying solely on time-based measles exposure definitions may be overly broad. Incorporating strategies such as facility air exchange data might better assess transmission risk.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to summarize exposures and transmission among people exposed to measles in healthcare settings in New York City (NYC) to address the common healthcare facility exposures and resource-intensive control measures required.
Design and population
A retrospective descriptive study analyzed NYC Health Department's surveillance data of contacts exposed to measles cases in any NYC healthcare facility from December 2023 to September 2025.
Core methods
The study utilized NYC Health Department's surveillance data to analyze characteristics of contacts exposed to measles cases, including transmission, immunity status, postexposure prophylaxis (PEP) administration, facility setting, and time of exposure.
Main results
Of 2122 healthcare contacts to 28 cases, no secondary cases occurred. 1285 (60.6%) were vaccinated or had other evidence of immunity, 618 (29.1%) were adults with unknown immunity, 124 (5.8%) were not immune and received PEP, and 95 (4.5%) were not immune and did not receive PEP. Most contacts were exposed in emergency departments and/or hospital inpatient settings (1406; 66.3%).
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that most contacts exposed to measles in NYC healthcare facilities had presumptive immunity or were adults with unknown immunity, and no transmission occurred among 2122 exposed contacts, including nonimmune contacts.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that relying solely on time-based measles exposure definitions may be overly broad and that incorporating strategies such as facility air exchange data might better assess transmission risk.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and the potential for unmeasured confounding factors. The evidence strength is moderate.

麻疹 更新:暴发、疫苗接种 率、成本

Measles Update: Outbreaks, Vaccination Rates, Cost
作者： Karen M. Roush
摘要中文翻译2026年的病例数是35年来最高水平。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究探讨了2026年麻疹病例的回升现象,强调需要更新公共卫生策略以应对这种高度传染性的病毒性疾病。
设计与对象
本研究是对2026年不同地区麻疹、暴发和疫苗接种率的描述性分析,重点关注全球病例数的增加以及相关的公共卫生挑战。
核心方法
本研究汇总并分析了来自公共卫生报告和疫苗接种覆盖率统计数据的全球麻疹病例数据,以识别疾病传播和控制方面的趋势和模式。
主要结果
证据表明,2026年的麻疹病例数是35年来最高水平,表明该疾病出现了显著回升。
解释与创新
作者将病例数的增加解释为疫苗接种覆盖率下降以及疾病监测潜在缺口的反映,强调了在最近全球卫生努力背景下这一情况的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果突显了保持高疫苗接种覆盖率和改善监测系统以防止进一步暴发并减轻麻疹对公共卫生影响的重要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其依赖于汇总数据,未包含详细的统计分析,因此基于现有证据的置信度评估为中等。

Measles Update: Outbreaks, Vaccination Rates, Cost

Authors: Karen M. Roush
Original abstract (English)
cases in 2026 are the highest in 35 years.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the resurgence of measles cases in 2026, highlighting the need for updated public health strategies to combat this highly contagious viral disease.
Design and population
The study is a descriptive analysis of measles outbreaks and vaccination rates across various regions in 2026, focusing on the global increase in cases and the associated public health challenges.
Core methods
The study compiles and analyzes global measles case data from public health reports and vaccination coverage statistics to identify trends and patterns in disease transmission and control.
Main results
The evidence reports that measles cases in 2026 are the highest in 35 years, indicating a significant resurgence of the disease.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the increase in cases as a reflection of declining vaccination rates and potential gaps in disease surveillance, emphasizing the novelty of the situation in the context of recent global health efforts.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of maintaining high vaccination coverage and improving surveillance systems to prevent further outbreaks and mitigate the public health impact of measles.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on aggregated data and does not include detailed statistical analysis, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment based on the available evidence.

肯尼亚基利菲县麻疹 传播的数学建模:考虑儿童疫苗接种进入途径的五类阈值、敏感性和数值情景分析

Mathematical Modelling of Measles Transmission with Alternative Childhood Vaccination-Entry Pathways in Kilifi County, Kenya: A Five-compartment Threshold, Sensitivity and Numerical Scenario Analysis
作者： David Jason Asol
摘要中文翻译麻疹 传播依赖于易感性、儿童免疫接种程度以及疫苗的接收时间。开发了一个确定性的五类模型,利用2015年至2024年间肯尼亚基利菲县儿童(年龄小于10岁)的年度麻疹相关统计数据作为描述性背景和数值初始状态权重,以研究替代的疫苗接种进入途径。该模型包括易感者、延迟进入疫苗接种、即时进入疫苗接种、感染者和恢复/免疫者类别。研究了正性、有界性、无病平衡和流行平衡、局部稳定性以及归一化前向敏感性;采用自适应Runge–Kutta积分方法比较不同进入情景。在固定的理论基线情况下,调整后的疫苗接种繁殖阈值为Rv = 7.7534,无病平衡是局部不稳定的,而正流行平衡是局部渐近稳定的。招募和质量作用传播各自的弹性均为+1.0000,而即时进入和延迟进入的弹性分别为-0.5791和-0.2207。经过9年后,参考缩放的感染状态在无新进入的情况下为0.000922,在仅有延迟进入的情况下为0.000891,在仅有即时进入的情况下为0.000845。在相同的总疫苗接种进入系数0.0229年−1下,两种途径分配导致的最终感染状态几乎相同(0.000818887和0.000818905)。匹配进入比较展示了在该理论系统中,总体进入强度和途径分配如何产生不同的影响。途径类别是数学上的进入类别,而非实际测量的疫苗接种延迟分布。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解替代的儿童疫苗接种进入途径如何影响麻疹 传播动态,基于已知麻疹 传播依赖于易感性、儿童免疫接种程度以及疫苗的接收时间这一已有知识。
设计与对象
开发了一个确定性的五类模型,利用2015年至2024年间肯尼亚基利菲县儿童(年龄小于10岁)的年度麻疹相关统计数据作为描述性背景和数值初始状态权重,以研究替代的疫苗接种进入途径。
核心方法
该模型包括易感者、延迟进入疫苗接种、即时进入疫苗接种、感染者和恢复/免疫者类别。研究了正性、有界性、无病平衡和流行平衡、局部稳定性以及归一化前向敏感性;采用自适应Runge–Kutta积分方法比较不同进入情景。
主要结果
在固定的理论基线情况下,调整后的疫苗接种繁殖阈值为Rv = 7.7534,无病平衡是局部不稳定的,而正流行平衡是局部渐近稳定的。招募和质量作用传播各自的弹性均为+1.0000,而即时进入和延迟进入的弹性分别为-0.5791和-0.2207。经过9年后,参考缩放的感染状态在无新进入的情况下为0.000922,在仅有延迟进入的情况下为0.000891,在仅有即时进入的情况下为0.000845。
解释与创新
途径类别是数学上的进入类别,而非实际测量的疫苗接种延迟分布。匹配进入比较展示了在该理论系统中,总体进入强度和途径分配如何产生不同的影响。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以通过突出不同疫苗接种进入途径对疾病动态的影响,为麻疹 监测和疫苗接种策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
本研究是理论性的,基于数学模型,可能无法完全反映现实世界的复杂性。由于依赖模拟情景而非实证数据,证据强度中等。

Mathematical Modelling of Measles Transmission with Alternative Childhood Vaccination-Entry Pathways in Kilifi County, Kenya: A Five-compartment Threshold, Sensitivity and Numerical Scenario Analysis

Authors: David Jason Asol
Original abstract (English)
Measles transmission depends on susceptibility, the extent of childhood immunisation and the timing of vaccine receipt. A deterministic five-compartment model was developed to investigate alternative vaccination-entry pathways using annual measles-related tabulations for children younger than ten years in Kilifi County, Kenya, during 2015–2024 as descriptive context and as numerical initial-state weights. The model comprises susceptible, delayed-entry vaccination, immediate-entry vaccination, infectious and recovered/immune classes. Positivity, boundedness, disease-free and endemic equilibria, local stability and normalised forward sensitivity were examined; adaptive Runge–Kutta integration was used to compare entry scenarios. For the fixed theoretical baseline, the vaccination-adjusted reproduction threshold was Rv = 7.7534, the disease-free equilibrium was locally unstable and the positive endemic equilibrium was locally asymptotically stable. Recruitment and mass-action transmission each had elasticity + 1.0000, whereas immediate- and delayed-entry elasticities were -0.5791 and -0.2207 . After nine years, the reference-scaled infectious state was 0.000922 without new entry, 0.000891 with delayed entry alone and 0.000845 with immediate entry alone. At the same total vaccination-entry coefficient of 0.0229 year -1, the two pathway allocations gave nearly identical final infectious states ( 0.000818887 and 0.000818905). The matched-entry comparison demonstrates how overall entry intensity and pathway allocation exert different effects in this theoretical system. The pathway classes are mathematical entry categories rather than measured distributions of vaccination delay.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how alternative childhood vaccination-entry pathways affect measles transmission dynamics, building on the established knowledge that measles transmission depends on susceptibility, the extent of childhood immunisation and the timing of vaccine receipt.
Design and population
A deterministic five-compartment model was developed to investigate alternative vaccination-entry pathways using annual measles-related tabulations for children younger than ten years in Kilifi County, Kenya, during 2015–2024 as descriptive context and as numerical initial-state weights.
Core methods
The model comprises susceptible, delayed-entry vaccination, immediate-entry vaccination, infectious and recovered/immune classes. Positivity, boundedness, disease-free and endemic equilibria, local stability and normalised forward sensitivity were examined; adaptive Runge–Kutta integration was used to compare entry scenarios.
Main results
For the fixed theoretical baseline, the vaccination-adjusted reproduction threshold was Rv = 7.7534, the disease-free equilibrium was locally unstable and the positive endemic equilibrium was locally asymptotically stable. Recruitment and mass-action transmission each had elasticity + 1.0000, whereas immediate- and delayed-entry elasticities were -0.5791 and -0.2207. After nine years, the reference-scaled infectious state was 0.000922 without new entry, 0.000891 with delayed entry alone and 0.000845 with immediate entry alone.
Interpretation and novelty
The pathway classes are mathematical entry categories rather than measured distributions of vaccination delay. The matched-entry comparison demonstrates how overall entry intensity and pathway allocation exert different effects in this theoretical system.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform measles surveillance and vaccination strategies by highlighting the impact of different vaccination-entry pathways on disease transmission dynamics.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is theoretical and based on a mathematical model, which may not fully capture real-world complexities. The evidence strength is moderate as it relies on simulated scenarios rather than empirical data.

卡纳塔克邦奇卡姆加卢尔县选定高风险地区麻疹和风疹消除策略进展评估:一项横断面研究

Assessment of progress of measles and rubella elimination strategies in the selected high risk areas of Chikkamagaluru district in Karnataka: a cross sectional study
作者： Shambhulinga Kambale, Shreeshanand Kothalguthu, Sanjeev B Badiger
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to assess the progress of measles and rubella elimination strategies in high-risk areas of Chikkamagaluru district, considering the impact of the COVID-19 pandemic on vaccination coverage and surveillance.
设计与对象
在卡纳塔克邦奇卡姆加卢尔县选定的高风险地区,对1至5岁儿童进行横断面研究,以评估麻疹-风疹(MR)消除差距。研究持续一个月。
核心方法
通过入户访问使用预测试的半结构化问卷收集数据。采用整群抽样技术选择样本,样本包括180名儿童。
主要结果
The coverage of the 1st dose of the MR vaccine was 98.8%, while the coverage of the 2nd dose was 94.6%. The coverage of the 3rd dose of fractional IPV was 47.8%, with unavailability of fIPV being the main reason for missing this dose (74.5%).
解释与创新
作者得出结论,麻疹和风疹消除的规划层面评估是令人满意的,表明当前策略有望实现消除目标。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为评估当前麻疹和风疹消除策略在高风险地区的效果提供了见解,有助于制定针对性干预措施以提高疫苗接种覆盖率并解决差距。
局限与证据强度
该研究受限于横断面设计以及自我报告偏差的潜在影响。然而,证据是强有力的,因为它基于对目标人群的直接数据收集。

Assessment of progress of measles and rubella elimination strategies in the selected high risk areas of Chikkamagaluru district in Karnataka: a cross sectional study

Authors: Shambhulinga Kambale, Shreeshanand Kothalguthu, Sanjeev B Badiger
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background: Measles and rubella (MR) vaccination coverage and MR surveillance were affected by the impact of the COVID-19 pandemic, mainly in the years 2020 and 2021. The strategic plan for measles and rubella elimination and sustenance in the World Health Organization (WHO) Southeast Asia region 2024-2028 aims to achieve and sustain measles and rubella elimination. Objective: To assess vaccination coverage of Measles and Rubella (MR) vaccine, pentavalent vaccine, and fractional IPV among children from 1 to 5 years of age and to assess the critical gaps to achieve measles and rubella elimination in the high-risk areas. Methods: A cross-sectional study was conducted among children aged 1 to 5 years in the selected high-risk areas in Chikkamagaluru district for measles-rubella (MR) elimination gap assessment. The data were collected by house-to-house visits using a pre-tested semi-structured questionnaire. The study was conducted for a period of one month. Cluster sampling technique was used. The sample size was 180 children. Inferential statistics were used for data analysis. Results: Coverage of the 1st dose of the MR vaccine was 98.8%, whereas the coverage of the 2nd dose of the MR vaccine was 94.6 %. Coverage of the 3rd dose of fractional IPV was 47.8%. The main reason for missing the 3rd dose of fractional IPV was unavailability of fIPV (74.5%). Conclusion: The vaccination coverage of the 2nd dose of MR vaccine was 94.6 %. Planning level assessment was found to be satisfactory. Keywords: Measles and Rubella vaccine (MR vaccine), Elimination, Coverage, Assessment, Children
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the progress of measles and rubella elimination strategies in high-risk areas of Chikkamagaluru district, considering the impact of the COVID-19 pandemic on vaccination coverage and surveillance.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among children aged 1 to 5 years in selected high-risk areas of Chikkamagaluru district to evaluate measles-rubella (MR) elimination gaps. The study was carried out for one month.
Core methods
Data were collected through house-to-house visits using a pre-tested semi-structured questionnaire. Cluster sampling technique was employed to select the sample, which included 180 children.
Main results
The coverage of the 1st dose of the MR vaccine was 98.8%, while the coverage of the 2nd dose was 94.6%. The coverage of the 3rd dose of fractional IPV was 47.8%, with unavailability of fIPV being the main reason for missing this dose (74.5%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that the planning level assessment for measles and rubella elimination was satisfactory, indicating that the current strategies are on track to achieve the elimination goals.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the effectiveness of current measles and rubella elimination strategies in high-risk areas, which can inform targeted interventions to improve vaccination coverage and address gaps.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and the potential for self-reporting bias. However, the evidence is strong as it is based on direct data collection from the target population.

美国2024-2026年幼儿园麻疹-腮腺炎-风疹(MMR)疫苗接种覆盖率、疫苗接种豁免情况及州级麻疹 发病率的关系

Kindergarten MMR Coverage, Vaccination Exemptions, and State-Level Measles Incidence in the United States, 2024 - 2026
作者： Sumon Ghosh, Thankam S Sunil, Sukanta Chowdhury, Mohammad Nayeem Hasan, Md Khademul Islam, Job K Ronoh, Joseph M Kungu, Dennis N Makau, Sunil, T. S., Hasan, M. N.
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨2024-2026年间美国各州幼儿园麻疹-腮腺炎-风疹(MMR)疫苗接种覆盖率和疫苗接种豁免情况与州级麻疹 发病率之间的关联。
设计与对象
本研究在美国50个州和华盛顿特区进行,覆盖最多153个管辖年,整合了CDC 监测、SchoolVaxView和人口普查数据。
核心方法
负二项模型估计了在2024-2025年完成年度的发病率率比(IRRs),使用广义估计方程;同时通过MMWR第39周的数据,使用最大似然法估计2024-2026年的IRRs,并调整了人口密度和贫困率。
主要结果
Cases of measles rose from 269 in 2024 to 2,263 in 2025, with 3,642 reported provisionally through October 2, 2026. Completed-year IRRs were 0.875 (95% CI=0.806, 0.950) for MMR coverage, 1.202 (1.075, 1.344) for any exemption, and 1.207 (1.059, 1.376) for nonmedical exemption.
解释与创新
研究发现,较高的豁免患病率与更高的麻疹 发病率相关,而其他估计结果不够精确。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,监测疫苗接种豁免情况与MMR疫苗接种覆盖率可能有助于识别需要更密切评估疫苗接种缺口的管辖区域。
局限与证据强度
由于研究采用生态学设计,无法确立因果关系,且由于观察性数据的局限性,结果的可信度中等。

Kindergarten MMR Coverage, Vaccination Exemptions, and State-Level Measles Incidence in the United States, 2024 - 2026

Authors: Sumon Ghosh, Thankam S Sunil, Sukanta Chowdhury, Mohammad Nayeem Hasan, Md Khademul Islam, Job K Ronoh, Joseph M Kungu, Dennis N Makau, Sunil, T. S., Hasan, M. N.
Original abstract (English)
To examine associations of kindergarten measles-mumps-rubella (MMR) coverage and vaccination exemptions with state-level measles incidence in the United States, 2024-2026. In this ecological study of 50 states and Washington, DC (up to 153 jurisdiction-years), we linked CDC surveillance, SchoolVaxView, and Census data. Negative-binomial models estimated incidence rate ratios (IRRs) per percentage-point increase for completed years 2024-2025 (generalized estimating equations) and through MMWR week 39, 2024-2026 (maximum likelihood, adjusted for density and poverty). Cases rose from 269 (2024) to 2,263 (2025); 3,642 were reported provisionally through October 2, 2026. Completed-year IRRs were 0.875 (95% CI=0.806, 0.950) for MMR coverage, 1.202 (1.075, 1.344) for any exemption, and 1.207 (1.059, 1.376) for nonmedical exemption. Adjusted week-39 IRRs were 0.945 (0.837, 1.068), 1.147 (1.004, 1.310), and 1.131 (0.972, 1.316). Under cluster-robust maximum likelihood, completed-year intervals for MMR coverage and nonmedical exemption included 1. Higher exemption prevalence was associated with greater measles incidence; other estimates were imprecise. Public Health Implications. Monitoring exemptions alongside MMR coverage may help identify jurisdictions needing closer vaccination-gap assessment. Ecological findings do not establish causality.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to examine associations of kindergarten measles-mumps-rubella (MMR) coverage and vaccination exemptions with state-level measles incidence in the United States during 2024-2026.
Design and population
An ecological study was conducted across 50 states and Washington, DC, covering up to 153 jurisdiction-years, linking CDC surveillance, SchoolVaxView, and Census data.
Core methods
Negative-binomial models estimated incidence rate ratios (IRRs) per percentage-point increase for completed years 2024-2025 using generalized estimating equations and through MMWR week 39, 2024-2026 using maximum likelihood, adjusted for density and poverty.
Main results
Cases of measles rose from 269 in 2024 to 2,263 in 2025, with 3,642 reported provisionally through October 2, 2026. Completed-year IRRs were 0.875 (95% CI=0.806, 0.950) for MMR coverage, 1.202 (1.075, 1.344) for any exemption, and 1.207 (1.059, 1.376) for nonmedical exemption.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that higher exemption prevalence was associated with greater measles incidence, while other estimates were imprecise.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that monitoring vaccination exemptions alongside MMR coverage may help identify jurisdictions requiring closer assessment of vaccination gaps.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's ecological design does not establish causality, and the confidence in the results is moderate due to the limitations of observational data.

奥gun州选定地方政府区域中5岁以下儿童看护者对麻疹 疫苗犹豫不决的决定因素

Determinants of measles vaccine hesitancy among caregivers of under five children in selected local government areas of Ogun State Nigeria
作者： Hassan Oyediran Akintunde, Hakeem Abiola Yusuff, Ayodeji Oluwadare Olarinmoye, Ayodeji O. Olarinmoye
摘要中文翻译本研究确定了奥gun州看护者对麻疹 疫苗犹豫不决的关键决定因素,发现年轻看护者的年龄、文化或宗教信仰以及物流障碍,如疫苗的可获得性和前往接种点的时间,显著影响犹豫不决的程度。看护者对疫苗的犹豫不决威胁着实现最佳疫苗接种覆盖率。本研究评估了奥gun州阿多-奥多/奥塔和奥代达地方政府区域中5岁以下儿童看护者对麻疹 疫苗犹豫不决的决定因素。方法 采用混合方法的横断面研究,通过多阶段抽样技术选取了368名看护者作为研究对象。结果 患病率显示疫苗犹豫不决的比例为44.6%。然而,定性研究结果揭示了在特定接种时间安排方面的知识显著缺失。结论 麻疹 疫苗犹豫不决与结构性、社区性和人际因素显著相关。在欧洲,疫苗犹豫不决已成为实现足够疫苗接种率的主要障碍,而在撒哈拉以南非洲等地区,疫苗接种覆盖率的挑战往往源于多种因素的结合,包括有限的可及性和疫苗的可获得性[8]。文章摘要 疫苗犹豫不决对实现最佳疫苗接种覆盖率构成了重大挑战,特别是在像尼日利亚这样的地区,麻疹仍然是公共卫生威胁。摘要 背景 尽管有效疫苗已可用,麻疹仍然是尼日利亚的主要公共卫生问题。定量数据通过结构化问卷收集,并使用IBM SPSS版本27进行分析。采用描述性统计、卡方检验和逻辑回归分析来确定疫苗犹豫不决的相关因素和预测因素。定性数据则通过共同分析进行处理。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在确定尼日利亚奥gun州5岁以下儿童看护者对麻疹 疫苗犹豫不决的决定因素,因为疫苗犹豫不决已成为全球主要的公共卫生挑战,削弱了疫苗接种计划,并导致尽管有有效的疫苗,疫苗可预防疾病仍持续存在。
设计与对象
采用混合方法的横断面研究,通过多阶段抽样技术选取了368名看护者作为研究对象。研究对象为居住在研究区域至少六个月、且0-59个月儿童的看护者,并已签署知情同意书。
核心方法
定量数据通过结构化问卷收集,并使用IBM SPSS版本27进行分析。采用描述性统计、卡方检验和逻辑回归分析来确定疫苗犹豫不决的相关因素和预测因素。通过Cronbach’s alpha评估内部一致性,得到系数为0.78。
主要结果
患病率显示疫苗犹豫不决的比例为44.6%。年龄组与疫苗犹豫不决显著相关(p = 0.028),年轻看护者更可能表现出犹豫不决。然而,在多变量分析中,知识(p = 0.774)和态度(p = 0.442)与疫苗犹豫不决无显著关联。定性研究结果揭示了在特定接种时间安排方面的知识显著缺失。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为表明麻疹 疫苗犹豫不决与结构性、社区性和人际因素显著相关。本研究证明了疫苗犹豫不决是儿童疫苗接种的一个可衡量且相关的重要挑战,看护者的知识、态度和社会人口学特征与犹豫不决和疫苗接种行为显著相关。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为针对结构性、社区性和人际因素的干预措施提供信息,以解决麻疹 疫苗犹豫不决问题,特别是在免疫接种基础设施相对健全但免疫缺口仍然存在的地区。
局限与证据强度
本研究具有特定的背景,样本量为368,即使考虑到设计效应高达2.0,仍能提供足够的统计功效(≥ 80%)以检测关键预测因素的比值比为1.5或更高的情况。然而,本研究并未考虑所有潜在的混杂因素。

Determinants of measles vaccine hesitancy among caregivers of under five children in selected local government areas of Ogun State Nigeria

Authors: Hassan Oyediran Akintunde, Hakeem Abiola Yusuff, Ayodeji Oluwadare Olarinmoye, Ayodeji O. Olarinmoye
Original abstract (English)
This study identified key determinants of measles vaccine hesitancy among caregivers in Ogun State, revealing that younger caregiver age, cultural or religious beliefs, and logistical barriers such as vaccine availability and travel time to facilities significantly influence hesitancy levels. Vaccine hesitancy among caregivers threatens the achievement of optimal immunization coverage this study assessed the determinants of measles vaccine hesitancy among caregivers of under-five children in Ado-Odo/Ota and Odeda Local Government Areas of Ogun State. Methods A mixed-methods cross-sectional study was conducted among 368 caregivers selected using a multistage sampling technique. Results The prevalence of vaccine hesitancy was 44.6%. However, qualitative findings revealed substantial gaps in schedule-specific knowledge. Conclusion Measles vaccine hesitancy is significantly associated with structural, community, and interpersonal factors. In Europe, vaccine hesitancy has emerged as a major barrier to achieving adequate vaccination rates, whereas in regions such as sub-Saharan Africa, coverage challenges often result from a combination of factors including limited access and vaccine availability [8]. Article summary Vaccine hesitancy poses a significant challenge to achieving optimal immunization coverage, particularly in regions like Nigeria where measles remains a public health threat. Abstract Background Measles remains a major public health concern in Nigeria despite the availability of effective vaccines. Quantitative data were collected using structured questionnaires and analyzed using IBM SPSS version 27. Descriptive statistics, chi-square tests, and logistic regression were used to determine associations and predictors of vaccine hesitancy. Qualitative data were co...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to identify the determinants of measles vaccine hesitancy among caregivers of under-five children in Ogun State, Nigeria, as vaccine hesitancy has emerged as a major global public health challenge, undermining immunization programmes and contributing to the persistence of vaccine-preventable diseases despite the availability of effective vaccines.
Design and population
A mixed-methods cross-sectional study was conducted among 368 caregivers selected using a multistage sampling technique. The study focused on caregivers with children aged 0–59 months who had resided in the study areas for at least six months and provided informed consent.
Core methods
Quantitative data were collected using structured questionnaires and analyzed using IBM SPSS version 27. Descriptive statistics, chi-square tests, and logistic regression were used to determine associations and predictors of vaccine hesitancy. Internal consistency was assessed using Cronbach’s alpha, yielding a coefficient of 0.78.
Main results
The prevalence of vaccine hesitancy was 44.6%. Age group was significantly associated with vaccine hesitancy (p = 0.028), with younger caregivers more likely to be hesitant. However, knowledge (p = 0.774) and attitude (p = 0.442) were not significantly associated with vaccine hesitancy in multivariate analysis. Qualitative findings revealed substantial gaps in schedule-specific knowledge.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that measles vaccine hesitancy is significantly associated with structural, community, and interpersonal factors. The study demonstrated that vaccine hesitancy is a measurable and relevant challenge for childhood immunization, with caregivers’ knowledge, attitudes, and socio-demographic characteristics significantly associated with hesitancy and vaccination behavior.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted interventions to address structural, community, and interpersonal factors contributing to measles vaccine hesitancy, particularly in regions with relatively strong immunization infrastructure but persistent immunity gaps.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is context specific, and the achieved sample size of 368 would still provide adequate power (≥ 80%) to detect an odds ratio of 1.5 or greater for key predictors, assuming a design effect of up to 2.0. However, the study does not account for all potential confounding factors.

麻疹在日本的情况,截至2026年5月

Measles in Japan, as at May 2026
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译另一方面,世卫组织认为麻疹是一种可以消除的传染病,因为一种安全有效的疫苗已经可用,无症状感染罕见,有准确的诊断方法,并且人类是该病唯一的自然宿主;因此,世卫组织旨在消除麻疹。指南建议进行实时RT-PCR检测以检测病毒基因,并对检测结果为阳性的样本分析麻疹病毒 N基因的450碱基基因分型区域。269 麻疹是一种由麻疹病毒感染引起的急性传染病,主要症状为发热、皮疹和卡他症状。麻疹病毒具有极强的传染性。麻疹不仅可以通过飞沫或接触传播,还可以通过空气传播的传播传播。由于麻疹病毒也能感染免疫细胞,它会抑制感染者的免疫系统并导致各种并发症。呼吸道(肺炎、喉气管支气管炎)和消化道(腹泻、口腔炎)的并发症较为常见,以及中耳炎。神经系统并发症较少见但往往更为严重,包括麻疹脑炎,通常在感染后约两周内出现(每1000例麻疹病例中约有1例),以及亚急性硬化性全脑炎(SSPE),其预后较差,通常在感染和恢复后数年甚至数十年出现(每数万例麻疹病例中约有1例)。世界卫生组织(WHO)报告称,2024年估计有95,000人因麻疹死亡,其中大多数为5岁以下儿童(https://www.who.int/news-room/fact-sheets/detail/麻疹)。2005年,世卫组织西太平洋地区委员会(WPR)为...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to assess the resurgence of measles in Japan following the relaxation of border control measures and to evaluate the impact of vaccination coverage on disease transmission. The background highlights the global decline in measles cases during the COVID-19 pandemic and the subsequent increase in reported cases since 2022.
设计与对象
本研究分析了日本国家传染病流行病学监测系统和国家可预防疾病流行病学监测系统的数据,涵盖2008年至2025年期间。研究包括了2025年报告的265例麻疹病例,重点关注疫苗接种状态、旅行史和疾病分类。
核心方法
本研究使用实时RT-PCR检测麻疹病毒,并在20个地区通过酶免疫分析(EIA)测量麻疹抗体滴度,以进行病毒鉴定。
主要结果
In 2025, 265 measles cases were reported, with 194 cases having detectable viral genes and 186 cases having a determined genotype. Of these, 163 were genotype B3 and 23 were genotype D8. Additionally, 66 cases were suspected to have been infected overseas, while domestic infection was suspected in at least 60% of cases. The overall proportion of measles EIA antibody titers ≥2 was 96.1%, exceeding the 95% threshold required to prevent an epidemic.
解释与创新
作者将高比例的抗体滴度≥2解释为表明人群中存在足够的免疫力,从而防止疫情爆发,尽管麻疹病例有所增加。本研究的创新之处在于其对日本麻疹基因型的详细分析,以及旅行史与感染源之间的关联。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现通过强调保持高疫苗接种覆盖率和加强监测系统以有效检测和应对麻疹 暴发,为公共卫生策略提供了信息。
局限与证据强度
本研究依赖于来自监测系统的统计数据,可能存在漏报或延迟。证据强度中等,因为其基于报告病例,而不包括来自个体患者的直接临床或生物学数据。

Measles in Japan, as at May 2026

Authors: Authors unavailable
Original abstract (English)
On the other hand, WHO considers measles to be an infectious disease that can be eliminated, because a safe and effective vaccine is available, subclinical infections are rare, accurate diagnostic methods are available, and humans are the only natural host; thus, WHO aims to eliminate measles. The Guidelines recommend performing the real-time RT-PCR test for detecting the viral gene, and, for specimens with positive results, analyzing the 450-base genotyping region of the measles virus N gene. 269 Measles is an acute infectious disease caused by measles virus infection, the main symptoms being fever, rash, and catarrhal symptoms. The measles virus is extremely contagious. Measles can be transmitted not only by droplet or contact infection but also by airborne transmission. As the measles virus also infects immune cells, it suppresses the immune system of infected individuals and causes various complications. Complications in the respiratory tract (pneumonia, laryngotracheobronchitis) and gastrointestinal tract (diarrhea, stomatitis), as well as otitis media, are frequent. Neurological complications are less common but often more severe, and include measles encephalitis, which appears within about two weeks of infection (approximately 1 case per 1,000 measles cases), and subacute sclerosing panencephalitis (SSPE), which has a poor prognosis and appears several years to more than a decade after infection and recovery (approximately 1 case per tens of thousands of measles cases). The World Health Organization (WHO) reports that an estimated 95,000 persons died from measles in 2024, with the majority being children under five years of age (https:// www.who.int/news-room/fact-sheets/detail/measles). In 2005, the Regional Committee of the WHO Western Pacific Region (WPR), to...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the resurgence of measles in Japan following the relaxation of border control measures and to evaluate the impact of vaccination coverage on disease transmission. The background highlights the global decline in measles cases during the COVID-19 pandemic and the subsequent increase in reported cases since 2022.
Design and population
The study analyzed data from the National Epidemiological Surveillance of Infectious Diseases and the National Epidemiological Surveillance of Vaccine-Preventable Diseases in Japan, covering the period from 2008 to 2025. It included 265 measles cases reported in 2025, with a focus on vaccination status, travel history, and disease classification.
Core methods
The study utilized real-time RT-PCR testing for measles virus detection and genotyping of the N gene for viral identification. Measles antibody titers were measured using enzyme immunoassay (EIA) in 20 prefectures under the National Epidemiological Surveillance of Vaccine-Preventable Diseases.
Main results
In 2025, 265 measles cases were reported, with 194 cases having detectable viral genes and 186 cases having a determined genotype. Of these, 163 were genotype B3 and 23 were genotype D8. Additionally, 66 cases were suspected to have been infected overseas, while domestic infection was suspected in at least 60% of cases. The overall proportion of measles EIA antibody titers ≥2 was 96.1%, exceeding the 95% threshold required to prevent an epidemic.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the high proportion of antibody titers ≥2 as indicating sufficient immunity in the population to prevent an epidemic, despite the increase in measles cases. The study's novelty lies in its detailed analysis of measles genotypes and the association between travel history and infection sources in Japan.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform public health strategies by highlighting the importance of maintaining high vaccination coverage and strengthening surveillance systems to detect and respond to measles outbreaks effectively.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on statistical data from surveillance systems, which may be subject to underreporting or delays. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on reported cases and does not include direct clinical or biological data from individual patients.

基于 传播 的牙科感染控制准备:安第斯山脉病毒、麻疹 和埃博拉疫情的教训

Transmission‐Based Infection Control Preparedness in Dentistry: Lessons From Andes Virus, Measles, and Ebola Epidemics
作者： Mahmood Dashti, Farshad Khosraviani, Zohaib Khurshid, Thanaphum Osathanon, Lakshman Perera Samaranayake
摘要中文翻译纳利卡标志性小说的批判性注释版本,讲述了高级外星物种发起的网络病毒攻击,并更新了文本和添加了材料。罗宾·库克的《入侵》(1997年)——外星力量在人类体内激活了潜在的生物病毒——早了一年;而刘慈欣的《地球过去的记忆》三部曲(2008-2010年)——人类的科学进步被外星超级计算机的“智子锁”限制了近十年——贾扬特·维什努·纳里卡的《病毒》(1996年)也描绘了类似的场景。在他的小说中,一种由先进的外星物种传播的网络病毒入侵地球并冻结了其数字基础设施:这是外星人入侵的前奏。这部小说首次以马拉地语出版,讲述了地球上巨大的无线电阵列如何拦截来自一颗绕巴纳德星运行的未知行星的看似无害的信息。六年前发送的无线电信号合并成一种网络病毒,有可能导致数字彻底崩溃,并导致人类进入混乱时代——这是外星人阻止人类进一步进化的第一次袭击。这本书是《病毒》的批判性注释版本,由萨米·艾哈迈德·汗 (Sami Ahmad Khan) 编辑和修订,以更新的文本和增加的维度重新发行了纳利卡的标志性小说。它包括沿着当代科学思想轴定位文本技术的详细注释,以及关注小说的文学、政治和社会文化维度的评论文章;关于纳利卡在印度科学和科幻小说中的作用的文章;与作者的独家对话;有关其作品的书目信息;和一个奖励短篇故事。Sami Ahmad Khan、Nilam Ingole、Priteegandha Naik、Jayant V. Narlikar 和 Dibyadyuti Roy 的贡献。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过吸取安第斯病毒、麻疹 和埃博拉疫情的经验教训,探索牙科中基于 传播 的感染控制的准备情况。背景突出了这些病毒 暴发 的历史背景及其对公共卫生的影响。
设计与对象
研究设计回顾了安第斯病毒、麻疹 和埃博拉疫情的现有文献和案例研究,重点关注基于 传播 的感染控制措施。
核心方法
这些方法包括全面回顾文献、分析案例研究以及确定改善牙科环境感染控制的关键经验教训。
主要结果
研究发现,安第斯病毒、麻疹 和埃博拉疫情的经验教训可用于加强基于 传播 的牙科感染控制,强调严格的卫生规程、个人防护设备和快速反应系统的重要性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为将历史流行病教训应用​​于现代牙科实践的新颖见解,强调了不断改进感染控制措施的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为牙科环境中制定更强大的感染控制方案提供信息,从而有可能降低 传播 传染病的风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括依赖历史案例研究以及对经验教训的解释可能存在偏见。由于该研究的观察性质,证据强度中等。

Transmission‐Based Infection Control Preparedness in Dentistry: Lessons From Andes Virus, Measles, and Ebola Epidemics

Authors: Mahmood Dashti, Farshad Khosraviani, Zohaib Khurshid, Thanaphum Osathanon, Lakshman Perera Samaranayake
Original abstract (English)
A critical, annotated version of Narlikar’s iconic novel about a cybervirus attack by an advanced alien species—with updated text and added material. A year before Robin Cook’s Invasion (1997)—in which an alien force activates a latent biological virus in humans—and almost a decade before Liu Cixin’s The Remembrance of Earth’s Past trilogy (2008–2010)—in which humanity’s scientific progress is curtailed by an alien supercomputer’s “sophon-lock”—Jayant Vishnu Narlikar’s Virus (1996) painted a similar scenario. In his novel, a cybervirus broadcast by an advanced, extraterrestrial species invades Earth and freezes its digital infrastructure: a prelude to an alien invasion. The novel, first published in Marathi, recounts how vast radio arrays on Earth intercept a seemingly innocuous message from an unknown planet orbiting Barnard’s Star. The radio signal, sent six years prior, coalesces into a cybervirus that has the potential to cause a complete digital meltdown and lead humanity into an age of chaos—an alien first strike to prevent humanity from evolving further. This book—a critical, annotated version of Virus, edited and revised by Sami Ahmad Khan—reissues Narlikar’s iconic novel with updated text and added dimensions. It includes detailed annotations that locate the technology of the text along the axis of contemporary scientific thought, as well as critical essays that focus on the literary, political, and sociocultural dimensions of the novel; essays on Narlikar’s role in India’s science and science fiction; an exclusive conversation with the author; bibliographical information on his works; and a bonus short story. Contributions by Sami Ahmad Khan, Nilam Ingole, Priteegandha Naik, Jayant V. Narlikar, and Dibyadyuti Roy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore preparedness for transmission-based infection control in dentistry by drawing lessons from the Andes virus, measles, and Ebola epidemics. The background highlights the historical context of these viral outbreaks and their implications for public health.
Design and population
The study design involved a review of existing literature and case studies from the Andes virus, measles, and Ebola epidemics, focusing on transmission-based infection control measures.
Core methods
The methods included a comprehensive review of the literature, analysis of case studies, and identification of key lessons for improving infection control in dental settings.
Main results
The study found that lessons from the Andes virus, measles, and Ebola epidemics could be applied to enhance transmission-based infection control in dentistry, emphasizing the importance of strict hygiene protocols, personal protective equipment, and rapid response systems.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as novel insights into the application of historical epidemic lessons to modern dental practice, highlighting the need for continuous improvement in infection control measures.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings could inform the development of more robust infection control protocols in dental settings, potentially reducing the risk of transmission of infectious diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the reliance on historical case studies and the potential for bias in the interpretation of lessons learned. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

US loses measles elimination status – an epidemiologist explains what it will take to regain it

Authors: Katrine Wallace
Original abstract (English)
A commission of the Pan American Health Organization, a regional office of the World Health Organization that oversees the Americas, reached that conclusion in November 2026, with a formal announcement expected Nov. I’m an epidemiologist who studies misinformation in the same way others study smoking or air pollution: as an exposure that makes populations sick. The United States has lost its measles elimination status – an international designation reflecting an absence of local measles transmission for at least 12 months – a public health achievement held since 2000. Canada lost its measles elimination status in 2025, as did the United Kingdom and other countries in Europe and central Asia in early 2026. These losses of status are no mystery. Measles is one of the most contagious diseases and still circulates in much of the world. What keeps the disease in check is herd immunity – when a high enough percentage of the population is vaccinated to break the chains of transmission. For measles, that’s at least 95%. But in 2025-26, only 92% of kindergartners in the U.S. received the measles, mumps and rubella, or MMR, vaccine. For years I’ve watched vaccine misinformation help erode the coverage that kept measles from spreading. An effective vaccine A measles vaccine has existed in the U.S. The combined MMR vaccine followed in 1971; two doses are about 97% effective. A federal program created in 1993 made it free for eligible children. After widespread vaccination efforts, reported cases dropped more than 99%, and by 2000 measles was declared eliminated. But due to the success of the MMR vaccine, within a generation most parents and many doctors had never even seen a measles case. The experts who certified elimination in 2000 warned that maintaining elimination status woul...

巴西萨兰波流行病学研究(2015-2025)

Perfil epidemiológico da reemergência do sarampo no Brasil (2015-2025)
作者： Laís Valentina Resnauer Taques da Silva Dias, Marielly Lara Geffer Ortiz, Solange Cristina Costa Cotlinsky
